## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a consistent, disciplined template with action-style titles and subtitles, strong navy and blue contrast, and a clear route from foundation to priorities to business detail to success outcomes. Slides 5, 9 and 13 are readable and well organized, with the four numbered priorities on slide 5 an effective anchor and the stacked chart on slide 13 labeled directly with a clear growth callout. Weaknesses are consequential: several slides, such as 3, 10, 12 and 14, run small text and tiny chart labels that would be hard to read in a room, slide 9 is a dense text grid, and some slides rely on repeated gold boxes and generic claims rather than a single clear visual takeaway. The appendix is long, with small, dense text that is fine as reference, but the main body of 16 slides is only partly memorable.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent navy and blue template, the numbered four-priority layout on slide 5, and the labeled stacked bar with CAGR bracket on slide 13.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge the small text and chart labels on slides 3, 10, 12 and 14 and cut the dense bullet grid on slide 9 to fewer, larger statements.

**What would change the score:** I would make each body slide lead with one visual proof point rather than text boxes plus a gold summary box, and the sector charts on slides 7 and 12 should state their takeaway in the title.

_Confidence medium. 23 of 23 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide and then a 'strong foundation' overview of Prudential's five businesses: Group Insurance, Individual Life, Retirement, PGIM and International. It moves to a scale view with headline figures on customers, assets under management and capital returned, followed by a description of the integrated model and its distinctive advantages. Strategy is presented next, anchored on four priorities covering focus on chosen businesses, capital optimization, global scale efficiency and geographic choices. Subsequent slides examine secular demand trends, retirement market leadership by country, PGIM's credit platform, and the Group Insurance and Individual Life businesses with market share and sales forecasts. The deck then covers portfolio rotation, the redesigned financial profile with cost targets, and the multi-year path. It closes with success outcomes and a long appendix of footnotes, forward-looking statements and non-GAAP reconciliations. Slides worth studying include the strategic priorities slide, which pairs four numbered pillars with short bullets, the market-share slide for retirement, and the Individual Life sales chart.

## Questions about this deck

**What does the Prudential strategy update say about its strategic priorities?**

Slide 5 presents four priorities: making deliberate geographic choices, building scale in chosen businesses, optimizing capital deployment, and leveraging global scale to become more efficient. The efficiency priority targets $750M in pre-tax run-rate benefits by year-end 2028 through enterprise redesign.

**How many slides does the Prudential presentation have?**

The Prudential deck is listed as 23 pages in total. The slide text provided runs through slide 21, the Non-GAAP Measures page, with footnotes on slides 18 and 19 and a forward-looking statements page on slide 20.

**What market share and ranking positions does Prudential report in global retirement on slide 9?**

Slide 9 lists #4 in RILA, #1 in PRT, and #3 largest life insurer by inforce face amount. It also shows an 11% overall share of the Life & Annuity market and a top 3 longevity reinsurer position in the U.K. and Netherlands.

**What does the Prudential deck say about the PGIM credit platform?**

Slide 10 describes a Credit Platform with $1.2T in assets, including Private Credit at $270B and Public Credit at $946B. It also lists 5,400+ lending relationships and a top 5 active fixed income manager ranking globally.

**Text**

STRATEGY UPDATE / August 2026 / Prudential

**Layout**

Single left-aligned title composition on a full-bleed dark navy field. The title block sits at the vertical middle-left, with the Prudential logo in the top-left corner at about a 4% margin. A large decorative emblem fills the right half, bleeding off the right and bottom edges. There is no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

One decorative illustration: a large tonal Prudential emblem occupying the right side of the slide. It shows a thick circular ring (outer edge around x 46 to 100, y 12 to 100) enclosing a stylised angular silhouette of a rock-like or bird-like form with sharp facets and feather-like stripes, all in tonal blues. The emblem is cropped by the right and bottom slide edges and is rendered as flat fills with no shadow or gradient. It sits behind the title. The Prudential logo (a circular emblem with a stylised figure plus the wordmark 'Prudential' in a white serif) appears top-left in white.

**Design**

Background is a flat deep navy field. Title is set in a bold condensed sans-serif, all capitals, white (#FFFFFF), about 13.3% of slide height with generous tracking. Subtitle is a medium-weight condensed sans-serif in bright blue (#007BC3), about 4.4% of slide height, mixed case. The decorative emblem uses a slightly lighter navy (#00315E) on the #001F45 background. Lines and shapes are flat with no corner radii, shadows or gradients. Logo wordmark is a white serif.

**Layout**

Five equal-width columns span the slide between about 1.1% and 98.9% of the width, each about 19.1% wide with gaps of about 0.6%. Each column has a coloured header band with an icon and a column name, above a white body card with the description. The title sits top-left at x 3.8%, y 6.8%, with the subtitle beneath it at y 14.2%. A gold double-headed arrow banner crosses the lower part of the columns at y 75.3% to 88.6%, with its text centred inside. A small logo sits bottom-left and the page number sits bottom-right at x 95.8%, y 96%. All body text is centred within its column.

**Visuals**

Five white line icons, each centred in its column header band at about y 31% to 36%. PGIM: a banknote with a circle and a second note behind it, in white on navy. RETIREMENT: a shield outline with a check mark, in white on blue. GROUP INSURANCE: four stylised standing figures in a row, in navy on cyan. INDIVIDUAL LIFE: an open umbrella with a curved handle, in white on violet. INTERNATIONAL: a globe with meridian and latitude lines on a stand, in navy on grey. Below the columns, a gold (#E3B449) double-headed block arrow spans almost the full slide width, with pointed tips at both ends and a rectangular shaft. A small navy circular logo mark sits bottom-left. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with no gradients or shadows. Title in bold condensed sans-serif capitals (closest match PrudentialModern-BoldSem) in navy #001F45, about 6.7% of slide height. Subtitle in the same bold condensed sans in blue #007BC3, about 3.3% of slide height. Body text in medium-weight condensed sans, navy #001F45, about 2.6% of slide height, with blue #007BC3 highlights on the words individuals and institutions. Column bodies are white rectangles with a thin accent-coloured outline on the sides and bottom. The gold banner text is bold condensed sans in navy #001F45 at about 3.0%. Square corners throughout.

### Slide 3

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A segment earnings pie chart paired with large headline numbers gives an immediate picture of scale and mix.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the small text and chart labels on slides 3, 10, 12 and 14 and cut the dense bullet grid on slide 9 to fewer, larger statements.

**Text**

A GLOBAL LEADER IN FINANCIAL SERVICES TODAY / COMPETING FROM A POSITION OF SCALE, DIVERSIFICATION, AND FINANCIAL STRENGTH / TTM Earnings Contribution(1) / PGIM / 12% / Retirement / 25% / Group Insurance / 4% / Individual Life / 8% / U.S. Legacy / Products / 12% / International / 39% / $6.9 / billion / Prudential by the Numbers / 150+ / Years of Service / and Commitment / 50M / Customers / $1.5T / Asset Management Assets / under Management(2) / $3B+ / Highly Liquid / Assets(3) Target / ~$3B / of Capital Returned to / Shareholders in 2025 / 4% / 5-yr Annual Dividends / per Share CAGR(4) / Note: See Appendix for all sources and footnotes. / 3

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white page: a donut chart panel on the left (about x 5.8 to 48 percent) and a statistics panel on the right (about x 53 to 94 percent), each under a full-width navy header bar. The slide title sits top-left at about x 3.8, y 6.8 with a blue subtitle directly beneath it; margins are about 4 percent on the left and 6 percent on the right. Footer: a small navy logo at bottom-left, a grey note beside it at about x 9.2, y 94.6, and the page number 3 at bottom-right (about x 95.8, y 96).

**Visuals**

Donut chart with title TTM Earnings Contribution(1) in the navy header bar. Type: donut chart with a white centre hole. Centre label reads $6.9 billion. Values are printed on each segment as percentages of TTM earnings, clockwise from top: PGIM 12% (dark navy #001F45), Retirement 25% (blue #007BC3), Group Insurance 4% (cyan #14D6E0), Individual Life 8% (indigo #443BB2), U.S. Legacy Products 12% (light grey #D6D6D6), International 39% (slate grey #7E8FA1). The six values sum to 100 percent. No axes, no legend, no highlighted points; labels sit directly on or beside each segment. Segments are separated by thin white gaps. Right panel: six statistics in a light blue rounded panel, arranged in a 2 by 3 grid with no icons or photographs: 150+ Years of Service and Commitment; 50M Customers; $1.5T Asset Management Assets under Management; $3B+ Highly Liquid Assets Target; ~$3B of Capital Returned to Shareholders in 2025; 4% 5-yr Annual Dividends per Share CAGR. One small navy logo at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with navy and bright blue accents. Title in heavy condensed sans-serif capitals (PrudentialModern Bold-style) at about 6.7 percent of slide height, navy #001F45. Subtitle in the same condensed bold capitals at about 3.3 percent, blue #007BC3. Section header bars are solid navy #001F45 with white bold condensed text at about 3.0 percent. Statistic numbers are bold condensed in blue #007BC3 at about 5.9 percent; statistic labels are medium-weight sans-serif in navy #001F45 at about 2.6 percent. Chart labels are medium sans-serif at about 2.15 percent, white on dark segments and navy on light segments. Footnote text is light grey #717171 at about 1.5 percent; page number is slate #7E8FA1 at about 1.3 percent. Header bars have small corner radii of roughly 0.8 percent of slide width. The statistics panel is a pale blue #E5F1FF at 39 percent opacity with rounded corners of about 1.5 percent radius and no border or shadow. No gradients, no shadows.

### Slide 4

**Text**

WHAT SETS PRUDENTIAL APART
OUR UNIQUELY INTEGRATED MODEL CREATES A POWERFUL COMPETITIVE MOAT
Retirement
Asset Management
Protection
United States
Japan
Select Retirement Markets in Europe
Distinctive Advantages
✓ Formidable global brand
✓ Trust earned over a century
✓ Industry-leading liability generation capabilities across retirement, protection, and savings products globally
✓ World class asset management platform capable of sourcing, managing, and allocating capital across public and private markets
✓ Breadth of customer relationships and distribution reach
4

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide: a circular three-part diagram occupies the left half (about x 4 to 48%), and a pale-blue rounded panel with a gold-outlined rounded box holding the advantages list occupies the right half (x 51 to 95%). The title sits top-left at x 3.8%, y 6.8%, with the blue subtitle directly beneath it at y 14.2%. The bottom-left has a small logo and the bottom-right has the slide number, both on the same baseline near y 96%. Left-aligned text, margins of about 4% on the left and 4% on the right.

**Visuals**

Circular diagram on the left: a ring of three arc arrows around a two-lobed figure. The gold (#E3B449) Retirement arc runs up the left side from bottom-left to an arrowhead at top-centre-left, with the word Retirement set along it rotated. The navy (#001F45) Asset Management arc runs from top-centre clockwise down the right side to an arrowhead at lower-right, with the words Asset Management set along it rotated. The grey (#7E8FA1) Protection arc runs along the bottom from lower-right to an arrowhead at lower-left, with the word Protection set along it. The arcs are arranged clockwise Retirement, Asset Management, Protection. Inside the ring are two overlapping lobes: a mid-blue (about #3F8FCB) lobe labelled United States at upper-left, a light cyan (about #66DDE5) lobe labelled Japan at right, and a navy (#001F45) lobe at lower-left labelled Select Retirement Markets in Europe in white. The lobes overlap in a yin-yang style, and a white circle with the navy Prudential rock logo sits at the centre. Flat fills, no shadows, no gradients. Right side: a pale-blue (#F3F9FE) rounded rectangle panel with a gold (#E3B449) rounded-rectangle outline containing five navy check-mark bullet items. Small navy Prudential logo at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with navy and blue accents. Title in bold condensed sans-serif capitals in navy #001F45, approximately 6.7% of slide height. Subtitle in bold condensed sans-serif capitals in blue #007BC3, about 3.3% of slide height. Panel heading Distinctive Advantages in bold condensed sans-serif, navy #001F45, about 4.1%. Body text in a medium-weight humanist sans-serif, navy #001F45, about 3.0% of slide height. Check marks in Wingdings, navy. Page number about 1.3% in grey #7E8FA1. Panel is a pale blue #F3F9FE rounded rectangle with large corner radius. Gold rounded outline with large corner radius. Arcs are solid flat fills with arrowheads, no shadows.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Four numbered strategic pillars with consistent bullet structure make the priorities easy to scan in one view.
- Review: Slides 5, 9 and 13 are readable and well organized, with the four numbered priorities on slide 5 an effective anchor and the stacked chart on slide 13 labeled directly with a clear growth callout.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent navy and blue template, the numbered four-priority layout on slide 5, and the labeled stacked bar with CAGR bracket on slide 13.

**Text**

OUR STRATEGIC PRIORITIES
SCALING CHOSEN BUSINESSES, STRENGTHENING PERFORMANCE, AND INVESTING IN HIGHEST-CONVICTION OPPORTUNITIES
MAKING DELIBERATE GEOGRAPHIC CHOICES
1
✓ Reduce our retirement and insurance footprint of more than a dozen countries by roughly half
✓ Focus exclusively on large, developed markets
✓ Exit emerging markets
BUILDING SCALE IN OUR CHOSEN BUSINESSES
2
✓ Concentrate on global retirement, asset management, and select protection businesses
✓ Target both organic and inorganic sources of growth
✓ Become category leaders in chosen businesses
OPTIMIZING CAPITAL DEPLOYMENT
3
✓ Strengthen earnings contribution from capital-light businesses
✓ Pursue disciplined growth in retirement
✓ Manage product mix within each business to improve capital efficiency
LEVERAGING GLOBAL SCALE TO BECOME MORE EFFICIENT
4
✓ Further integrate our businesses to improve operating efficiency and speed of execution
✓ Achieve $750M in pre-tax run-rate benefits by year-end 2028 through enterprise redesign
✓ Increase operating leverage and drive sustainable improvement in earnings and free cash flow growth
STRATEGY ANCHORED ON FOUR PRIORITIES
5

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a narrow left column holds a vertically centred stacked heading, and a right column of four full-width horizontal bands sits from about x 22 to x 94. The title is top-left at x 3.8, with a blue subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned on a common margin of about 3.8 percent. Each band has a bold heading above it, a numbered circle overlapping its top-left corner, and three check-marked bullet lines inside the band. A small logo sits bottom-left and the page number sits bottom-right.

**Visuals**

None photographic. Four flat rounded-rectangle bands, stacked vertically with equal gaps, each with a numbered circle badge (1, 2, 3, 4) overlapping its top-left corner. Band 1 is dark navy #001F45, band 2 is process blue #007BC3, band 3 is bright cyan #14D6E0, band 4 is slate grey #7E8FA1. Each badge is a white circle with a coloured ring matching its band (navy, blue, cyan, grey) and the number printed in the same colour. Check marks are Wingdings tick glyphs. A small circular navy logo mark sits at the bottom-left corner.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and headings in navy #001F45 in a bold condensed-style sans-serif (PrudentialModern-BoldSem, all caps). Subtitle in process blue #007BC3, same family, all caps. Left-column heading in navy #001F45, same bold family. Band body text is medium-weight sans-serif (PrudentialModern-Medium); white on navy and blue bands, navy #001F45 on cyan and grey bands. Title is about 6.7 percent of slide height; subtitle and band headings about 3.3 percent; bullets about 2.6 percent; left heading about 5.2 percent; page number about 1.3 percent. Bands have small corner radii of about 1.2 percent of slide height, with flat fills and no shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Single-column composition on a white slide. The title sits top-left at about 3.8% from the left edge with a navy all-caps headline and a blue all-caps subtitle directly beneath it. **Layout**

Two-column layout on a white page: a bold all-caps title spans the top-left, with a blue all-caps subtitle directly beneath it. The left column holds a stacked bar chart under a navy header bar, and the right column holds three stacked gold-outlined market cards under a navy header bar, with a grey chevron arrow between the columns. A small logo and a grey source note sit at the bottom left, and the page number sits at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart with a trend arrow. Title: none printed, the navy header bar above it serves as its label. Columns (left to right): 2020, 2024, 2029P. Total labels above each column: $55T, $63T, $75T. Each column has a navy bottom segment and a blue top segment. Navy segment labels: 79% (2020), 80% (2024), 81% (2029P). Blue segment labels: 21% (2020), 20% (2024), 19% (2029P). Shares of total sum to 100% in each column. Y axis: none shown; no units or range printed. X axis: years 2020, 2024, 2029P, printed under the baseline. A thin light grey baseline runs under the columns. Annotation: a navy straight arrow line rises from just above the 2020 column to just below the top of the 2029P column, with an oval outline labelled 4% on the line above the 2024 column. Legend below the chart: navy square labelled U.S., Japan, Select Countries in Europe(2); blue square labelled Remaining Countries in the OECD(3). Highlighted points: none. Right-side graphic: a large grey (#7E8FA1) right-pointing chevron between the two columns. Three gold-outlined rounded rectangles stacked vertically, each containing a thin navy outline map: top, a map of the contiguous U.S.; middle, a map of Japan; bottom, a map of Europe with Scandinavia, the British Isles and the Mediterranean shown. Each map sits to the left of its label inside its card. Logo: a small navy circular emblem at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: plain white, no gradient or texture. Title: PrudentialModern Bold, a condensed sans-serif, all caps, navy #001F45, roughly 6.7% of slide height for the cap height block. Subtitle: PrudentialModern Bold condensed sans-serif, all caps, blue #007BC3, about 3.3% of slide height. Header bars: navy #001F45 fill with white PrudentialModern Bold text about 3.0% of slide height, rounded corners of roughly 0.5% of slide height radius, no shadow. Chart labels: PrudentialModern Medium sans-serif, navy #001F45 for totals and years, white for segment percentages, about 2.6% to 3.0% of slide height. Accents: blue #007BC3 segments and subtitle, grey #7E8FA1 chevron, gold outline on the three market cards (about #E8B33A), navy #001F45 trend line. Footer note: PrudentialModern Medium, grey #717171, about 1.5% of slide height. Page number: grey #7E8FA1, about 1.3% of slide height. Cards: white fill, gold outline about 0.4% of slide height wide, rounded corners, no shadow. Chevron: flat fill, no outline, no shadow.

**Layout**

Two equal columns of content sit side by side below a full-width title block, each column with a navy header bar above a pale rounded panel holding three stacked stat-and-caption groups centred in the panel. The title is flush left at the top margin with its blue subtitle directly beneath; a small logo sits bottom left with the note beside it, and the page number sits bottom right. Left column spans roughly x 9 to 45 percent, right column x 53 to 89 percent, with a wide gutter between.

**Visuals**

No photographs, charts, tables or diagrams. Only non-text elements are two pale rounded panels behind the stat columns, two navy header bars, and a small navy logo mark at bottom left. The logo is a small navy emblem with curved strokes, about 1.6 percent of slide width, with no other logos on the slide.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white slide with condensed bold grotesque sans-serif typography in all caps for the title and subtitle, and a bold condensed sans-serif for headers and stat figures. Title is navy #001F45, bold, about 6.7 percent of slide height. Subtitle is blue #007BC3, bold, about 3.3 percent of slide height. Header text is white #FFFFFF on navy #001F45 bars, about 3.0 percent of slide height. Stat figures are blue #0070C0, bold, about 5.9 percent of slide height. Panels are #E5F1FF at 39 percent opacity over white, appearing very pale blue, with rounded corners and no shadow. Header bars are #001F45 with rounded corners and no shadow. Note text is grey #717171 at about 1.5 percent; page number is grey-blue #7E8FA1 at about 1.3 percent.

### Slide 9

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Market opportunity and ranking figures are arranged in three country columns, pairing each market's position with its focus areas.
- Review: Slides 5, 9 and 13 are readable and well organized, with the four numbered priorities on slide 5 an effective anchor and the stacked chart on slide 13 labeled directly with a clear growth callout.
- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: several slides, such as 3, 10, 12 and 14, run small text and tiny chart labels that would be hard to read in a room, slide 9 is a dense text grid, and some slides rely on repeated gold boxes and generic claims rather than a single clear visual takeaway.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the small text and chart labels on slides 3, 10, 12 and 14 and cut the dense bullet grid on slide 9 to fewer, larger statements.

**Text**

DEEPENING LEADERSHIP IN GLOBAL RETIREMENT
LEVERAGING OUR DIFFERENTIATED CAPABILITIES TO STRENGTHEN AND BUILD LEADERSHIP POSITIONS
U.S. / Japan / Europe
Market / Opportunity
• Significant retirement savings, with a / substantial unmet demand for / protected lifetime income solutions
• Aging demographics, new government / initiatives, and a rising need for / products that help customers save and / convert assets into secure income
• Sweeping pension reforms / accelerating Defined Benefit unwind / and shift to bulk annuities and / pension consolidation
Primary Focus Areas
• Retail – Strengthen leadership in / annuity offerings / • Institutional – Continue to scale / Pension Risk Transfer opportunity in / ~$3T U.S. corporate pension market
• Captive Channel – Moving to a / stronger relationship-based model / focused on delivering long-term value / to customers / • Third-Party Channel – Strengthening / distribution in banks and independent / agencies
• Institutional – Build on leadership in / longevity reinsurance to address / growing demand for de-risking / solutions
Strong Starting / Positions in Largest / Retirement Markets
• #4 RILA(1) / • #1 PRT(2)
• #3 Largest Life Insurer by Inforce / Face Amount(3) / • 11% overall share of Life & Annuity / market(3)
• Top 3 longevity reinsurer / (U.K., Netherlands)(4)
Advancing leadership across our retirement businesses through enhanced / capabilities, market share gains, and greater operating leverage
Note: See Appendix for all sources and footnotes.
9

**Layout**

Full-width white slide with the title at top left, a blue subtitle directly beneath it, and a three-column grid (U.S., Japan, Europe) with three rows whose labels sit in navy chevron tabs at the left margin, which starts at about 3.8% of slide width. A gold-outlined takeaway box spans the bottom right, with the footnote and logo at bottom left and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

No photographs, charts or tables. Three navy right-pointing chevron shapes (arrow tabs with a point on the right side) label the rows: Market Opportunity, Primary Focus Areas, and Strong Starting Positions in Largest Retirement Markets. Thin light grey grid rules divide the columns and rows. A gold rounded-rectangle outline frames the takeaway sentence. A small navy circular corporate logo sits at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold condensed all-caps sans-serif (PrudentialModern-BoldSem style) in navy #001F45, about 6.7% of slide height. Subtitle in bold condensed all-caps in blue #007BC3, about 3.3%. Column headers in regular blue #0070C0, about 3.7%. Body text in regular sans-serif (PrudentialModern-Medium style) in navy #001F45, about 2.2%, with bullets in Arial-style navy. Chevron labels in bold white sans-serif about 3.0%. Chevrons are flat navy #001F45 with no shadow or gradient. Grid rules are light grey #C8CDD3, about 0.2% of slide height thick. Takeaway box has a gold #E6B43C outline about 0.4% thick, slightly rounded corners, white fill, no shadow. Takeaway text is regular navy #001F45 about 3.3%, centred. Footnote is grey #717171 about 1.5%; page number is grey-blue #7E8FA1 about 1.3%.

**Layout**

Two-column left panel with a full-width header band across the top of the left column: the title sits top-left at the margin, with the subtitle directly beneath it. A navy bar spans the left column, with a blue and a cyan box side by side beneath it, each holding a heading, an italic description and a bulleted Key Strategies list; a gold-outlined rounded panel at the right holds five stacked statistics centred in the panel. Footer note bottom-left beside a small logo, page number bottom-right, left margin about 4%.

**Visuals**

Non-text elements: (a) a solid navy horizontal bar (#001F45) with white bold heading 'Credit Platform – $1.2T in Assets' and a superscript (1); (b) a solid blue box (#007BC3) with white heading 'Private Credit – $270B'; (c) a solid bright cyan box (#14D6E0) with navy heading 'Public Credit – $946B'; (d) a large rounded rectangle with gold outline (#E7B33B approx., about 3 pt stroke, corner radius about 8% of panel width) and no fill, enclosing five statistics: 'Top 5' over 'Active Fixed Income Manager Globally(2)', '5,400+' over 'Lending Relationships', '2nd Largest' over 'Real Estate Manager(2)', '#2' over 'Player in Private Placements(2)', 'Top 10' over 'Global CLO Manager(3)'; (e) a small circular navy logo emblem (PGIM mark) at bottom-left. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background, flat with no gradient or shadow. Title navy #001F45 in heavy condensed sans-serif capitals (PrudentialModern Bold, close to a condensed grotesque), about 6.7% of slide height cap size. Subtitle bright blue #007BC3 in bold condensed capitals, about 3.3% of height. Panel headings: white on navy/blue, navy on cyan, bold condensed. Body text navy #001F45 in medium condensed sans, about 2.2% of height; descriptions in italic medium. Statistics in bold blue #0070C0 about 4.4% of height, captions navy medium about 2.6%. Footnote grey #717171 about 1.5%; page number grey-blue #7E8FA1 about 1.3%. Bars and boxes have square corners except the gold panel which is rounded. Bullets are small solid round dots.

### Slide 11

**Text**

FURTHER ADVANCE PGIM'S MARKET-LEADING FRANCHISE
BUILD ON STRENGTHS AND EXPAND INTO NEW ASSET CLASSES, GEOGRAPHIES, AND CLIENT CHANNELS
Scale and Broaden Capabilities
Grow leadership in credit and real assets,
scale asset-backed finance and direct lending, and
expand into primary private equity and infrastructure equity
Expand International Footprint
Roughly half of the $147T(1) global asset pool sits outside North America,
compared to only a quarter of PGIM's third-party AUM today
Diversify Client Base
Expanding where demand for private market solutions is accelerating
including insurance, sovereign wealth, and family office channels
Growing origination advantage to broaden investor base and drive
differentiated deal flow, third-party capital growth, and syndication earnings
Note: See Appendix for all sources and footnotes.
11

**Layout**

Single-column stacked layout with a left-aligned title and subtitle across the top, three rows of dark navy label boxes on the left, each paired with a blue arrow and a white outlined box on the right, and a centred gold-outlined callout box beneath. A small logo sits bottom-left with a grey note beside it, and the page number sits bottom-right. Margins are about 4 percent on the left and right.

**Visuals**

Three horizontal rows, each with a dark navy rounded rectangle (label) at left, a solid bright blue right-pointing triangle in the middle, and a white rounded rectangle with a blue outline at right. Row 1 label: Scale and Broaden Capabilities; right box: Grow leadership in credit and real assets, scale asset-backed finance and direct lending, and expand into primary private equity and infrastructure equity. Row 2 label: Expand International Footprint; right box: Roughly half of the $147T(1) global asset pool sits outside North America, compared to only a quarter of PGIM's third-party AUM today. Row 3 label: Diversify Client Base; right box: Expanding where demand for private market solutions is accelerating including insurance, sovereign wealth, and family office channels. Below the rows is a white rounded rectangle with a gold outline containing the callout sentence. Also a small navy circular PGIM-style logo at bottom left. No charts, photographs or icons beyond the logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with no gradient or texture. Title in bold condensed sans-serif capitals, navy #001F45, about 6.7 percent of slide height. Subtitle in bold condensed sans-serif capitals, blue #007BC3, about 3.3 percent of slide height. Label boxes use bold sans-serif in white on navy #001F45, about 3.0 percent of slide height. Right-hand box text is bold sans-serif in navy #001F45, about 2.6 percent. Callout text is medium-weight sans-serif in navy #001F45, about 3.3 percent. Accents: bright blue #007BC3 for arrows and outlines, gold #E8B83A for the callout outline. Outline strokes are about 0.3 percent of slide height with rounded corners of roughly 2 to 3 percent of height. No shadows.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Enlarge the small text and chart labels on slides 3, 10, 12 and 14 and cut the dense bullet grid on slide 9 to fewer, larger statements.
- What would change the score: I would make each body slide lead with one visual proof point rather than text boxes plus a gold summary box, and the sector charts on slides 7 and 12 should state their takeaway in the title.

**Text**

GROUP INSURANCE STRATEGY ANCHORED IN DIVERSIFICATION / INCREASING PRESENCE IN A LARGE AND GROWING ADDRESSABLE MARKET / Industry Product Mix / Other / Vision / Life / Disability / $200B(1) / Dental / Supp Health / Medical Stop Loss / Products we offer / Products we do not offer / Industry Segment Mix / Association / <500 lives / 5,000+ / lives / $200B(1) / 500 - 4,999 / lives / Pru segment maturity: / Significant / Moderate / Limited / #3 / Group Life and AD&D(2) / #6 / Group Disability(2) / KEY GROWTH OPPORTUNITIES / • Retaining our leadership position in the National market segment (5,000+ lives) while expanding in the Premier market / segment (100-5,000 lives) and in disability, absence management, and supplemental health / • Deepening employer and participant relationships to better cross-sell our expanding product portfolio / Note: See Appendix for all sources and footnotes. / 12

**Layout**

Two-column top band with two donut charts side by side and a summary panel at right, above a full-width rounded key-points box, with a footer note and page number at the bottom. Title is top-left at about 6.8% from top with a subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned at a 3.8% left margin. Slide number sits bottom-right at about 96% from top; the small logo sits bottom-left with the note beside it.

**Visuals**

Chart 1, donut, titled Industry Product Mix, centre label $200B(1). Seven segments clockwise from top: Life (navy #001F45, about 15% of ring), Disability (navy, about 12%), Supp Health (navy, about 5%), Medical Stop Loss (navy, about 15%), Dental (blue #007BC3, about 25%), Vision (blue, about 7%), Other (blue, about 5%). No values are printed; shares are estimates marked about. Navy means Products we offer, blue means Products we do not offer. Legend below the chart: navy swatch Products we offer, blue swatch Products we do not offer. Chart 2, donut, titled Industry Segment Mix, centre label $200B(1). Segments clockwise from top: Association (navy, about 1%), 5,000+ lives (navy, about 37%), 500 - 4,999 lives (blue #007BC3, about 47%), <500 lives (cyan #19D3DE, about 13%). No values printed; shares are estimates marked about. Legend in one row: Pru segment maturity: then navy swatch Significant, blue swatch Moderate, cyan swatch Limited. Highlight panel at right with two large statistics: #3 in blue #0070C0 above Group Life and AD&D(2), and #6 in blue above Group Disability(2). No photographs, logos other than the small footer mark, or icons other than that mark.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A stacked column chart of life insurance sales by product type, with a forecast callout, carries the growth argument directly.
- Review: Slides 5, 9 and 13 are readable and well organized, with the four numbered priorities on slide 5 an effective anchor and the stacked chart on slide 13 labeled directly with a clear growth callout.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent navy and blue template, the numbered four-priority layout on slide 5, and the labeled stacked bar with CAGR bracket on slide 13.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide: a stacked column chart with its legend sits in the left half under a navy label box, and a gold-outlined rounded box of growth bullets with a pale callout panel (Top 2 / #1) sits in the right half. The title is left-aligned at the top with a blue all-caps subtitle beneath it, both starting at about 3.8% from the left edge; the footnote and logo sit along the bottom-left and the page number sits at the bottom-right margin.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart titled Life Industry Sales ($B, All Carriers)(1) with two columns, 2025 (total $18.1B) and 2030F (total $21.7B); no y-axis or gridlines are shown, values are in $B printed inside segments. 2025 segments from bottom to top: Whole Life 6.3, Fixed Universal Life 0.9, Term Life 3.1, Indexed Universal Life 4.7, Variable Universal Life 3.1. 2030F segments from bottom to top: Whole Life 7.2, Fixed Universal Life 0.8, Term Life 3.4, Indexed Universal Life 6.3, Variable Universal Life 4.0. Legend at left, top to bottom: Variable Universal Life (cyan), Indexed Universal Life (blue), Term Life (navy), Fixed Universal Life (grey-blue), Whole Life (light grey). Small diagonal white break marks cross the Whole Life segments of both columns. A blue curly brace to the right of the 2030F column spans from its top to the top of its Fixed Universal Life segment, annotated ~5% CAGR in PRU’s focus areas (2025-2030F). A thin navy horizontal baseline runs under both columns with short tick ends. Also a rounded gold-outlined box, a navy label box, a pale blue callout panel with Top 2 and #1, and a navy Prudential rock logo at bottom-left. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Titles and most body text are navy #001F45; the subtitle, KEY GROWTH OPPORTUNITIES heading, CAGR annotation and Top 2 / #1 figures are blue (#007BC3 / #0070C0); the footnote is grey #717171 and the page number is grey-blue #7E8FA1. Accents: gold #E0B440 rounded outline around the growth box, cyan #14D6E0 and blue #007BC3 chart segments. Typeface is PrudentialModern family, sans-serif: Bold Semi-condensed for the title and subtitle (title about 6.7% of slide height, subtitle about 3.3%), Medium for body bullets and labels (about 2.6%), Bold for column year labels and totals. Shapes have rounded corners on the navy label box and the gold outline (radius about 8% of slide height); the pale callout panel is square-cornered with no border and no shadow.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Enlarge the small text and chart labels on slides 3, 10, 12 and 14 and cut the dense bullet grid on slide 9 to fewer, larger statements.

**Text**

ROTATING CAPITAL TO HIGHEST CONVICTION OPPORTUNITIES
MAKING INTENTIONAL CHOICES TO REDUCE OUR FOOTPRINT
Focus Markets
U.S.
Japan
Select Countries
in Europe
Asset
Management
PGIM
Retirement
PRT
Annuities
Retirement &
Investment
Products
PRT &
Longevity
Reinsurance
Protection
Group Insurance
Individual Life
Life Insurance
14

**Layout**

Single-slide matrix diagram on a white field: left-aligned title and blue subtitle sit in the top-left margin (x 3.8%), the 'Focus Markets' heading is centred above a large gold rounded rectangle that frames a 4-column by 3-row grid. Row labels occupy the left column (x 13.5% to 28%), three market column headers sit in the top row, and the bottom-left logo and bottom-right page number mark the footer.

**Visuals**

A matrix diagram (not a chart) with no photographs. Top row: three solid blue rounded rectangles labelled U.S., Japan and Select Countries in Europe, each about 14.4% wide and 10% tall at y 32.0%, x positions 32.0%, 51.5% and 71.0%. Left column: three solid navy rounded rectangles labelled Asset Management (y 45.4%), Retirement (y 60.2%) and Protection (y 74.9%), each 14.4% wide and 10% tall at x 13.6%. Grid cells: one wide white outlined rounded rectangle spanning x 32.0% to 85.4% at y 45.4% labelled PGIM; in the Retirement row (y 60.2%) three white outlined rounded rectangles labelled PRT Annuities (x 32.0%), Retirement & Investment Products (x 51.5%) and PRT & Longevity Reinsurance (x 71.0%); in the Protection row (y 74.9%) two white outlined rounded rectangles labelled Group Insurance Individual Life (x 32.0%) and Life Insurance (x 51.5%), with the x 71.0% cell empty. The outer gold rounded rectangle has no fill, a gold stroke and a very large corner radius. No arrows or connectors, no icons, no photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide with navy #001F45 as the dominant text and outline colour, bright blue #007BC3 as the accent for the subtitle and the three column headers, and gold #E5B54A as the frame colour. Titles are set in a bold condensed sans-serif (PrudentialModern-BoldSem, closest common match a condensed grotesque) in all caps; the main title is about 6.7% of slide height (cap height about 4.7%), the subtitle about 3.3%. Body and cell text is PrudentialModern-Medium, a regular-weight sans-serif at about 2.6% of slide height, and the navy row labels and column headers use the semibold weight at about 3.0%. Shapes have a navy outline about 0.3% of slide height thick on white cells, filled solid blue or navy on headers and row labels, corner radius about 1% of slide height on cells and about 6% on the outer frame. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 16

**Text**

A MULTI-YEAR JOURNEY, BUT THE PATH IS CLEAR / POSITIONING PRUDENTIAL TO DRIVE GREATER VALUE CREATION / Strengthening our financial profile and reinforcing the / AA ratings position we maintain today / Repositioning Prudential to be a more focused and / resilient company across market cycles / Creating strong financial outcomes, including top-quartile / earnings growth, excluding the earnings from our legacy variable / annuity business, and improving free cash flow conversion / 16

**Layout**

Single-column stack of three horizontal rows on a white field. Each row pairs a small filled rounded square icon tile on the left with a wide rounded outlined text box to its right; the icon tiles sit at about x 11.6% and the text boxes span x 27.6% to 88.4%. The title sits top-left at x 3.8% with a blue subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned. The page number sits bottom-right and a small logo sits bottom-left.

**Visuals**

Three filled rounded-square icon tiles, each about 12.9% wide and 15.2% tall, containing white line icons. Tile 1 (navy #001F45) holds a balance scale: a vertical post with a pivot circle on top, a horizontal beam, two hanging pans and a flat base. Tile 2 (blue #007BC3) holds a strategy board: a square clipboard-like frame with a spiral binding on its left edge, containing an X mark, an upward arrow, a circle and a second X connected by a curved path. Tile 3 (teal #14D6E0) holds a bar chart: an L-shaped axis frame with an upward diagonal arrow and three vertical bars of different heights. Icons are white, thin-stroked (about 2px), outline style. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title in heavy condensed sans-serif capitals (PrudentialModern Bold Semi) in navy #001F45, about 6.7% of slide height cap-size, tight tracking. Subtitle in the same condensed bold capitals in blue #007BC3, about 3.3% of slide height. Row body text is condensed bold sentence-case sans in navy #001F45, about 3.7% of slide height, centered. Accent colours are navy #001F45, blue #007BC3 and cyan #14D6E0, one per row. Row text boxes are white with 2px outlines in the matching accent colour (row 1 navy, row 2 blue, row 3 cyan) and corner radii of roughly 2% of slide width. Icon tiles have the same corner radius and no outline or shadow. No shadows.

### Slide 20

**Text**

FORWARD-LOOKING STATEMENTS
Certain of the statements included in this presentation, the 2Q26 Earnings Call presentation, and made during the earnings and strategy call, including those regarding our strategy, objectives, goals, priorities, expectations, plans, initiatives, or anticipated future performance, financial targets and objectives and capital priorities, planned transactions, actions to reduce costs and the expected impacts thereof, PGIM margin expansion, expected impacts to AOI of our annual assumption updates, our expectations for the pension risk transfer business, our expectations for earnings growth in the Individual Life business, the expected full year 2026 loss for our Corporate & Other segment, those under the heading “Seasonality of Key Financial Items,” trends and opportunities, the durability of our earnings profile, the expected duration, financial impact, including the expected impact to adjusted operating income, capital, economic solvency ratio and cash flow, and outcome of the Prudential of Japan sales suspension and the related remediation efforts, constitute forward-looking statements within the meaning of the U.S. Private Securities Litigation Reform Act of 1995. Words such as “expects”, “believes”, “anticipates”, “includes”, “plans”, “assumes”, “estimates”, “projects”, “intends”, “should”, “will”, “shall”, or variations of such words are generally part of forward-looking statements. Forward-looking statements are made based on management’s current expectations and beliefs concerning future developments and their potential effects upon Prudential Financial, Inc. and its subsidiaries. Prudential Financial, Inc.’s actual results may differ, possibly materially, from expectations or estimates reflected in such forward-looking statements. Certain important factors that could cause actual results to differ, possibly materially, from expectations or estimates reflected in such forward-looking statements include, among others, that our remediation efforts in Japan may be unsuccessful or take longer than we expect, that we may uncover additional misconduct, that the sales suspension at Prudential of Japan may continue for longer than we expect, losses on investments or financial contracts due to deterioration in credit quality or value, or counterparty default; losses on insurance products due to mortality experience, morbidity experience or policyholder behavior experience that differs significantly from our expectations when we price our products. Additional factors and uncertainties that could cause actual results to differ can be found in the “Risk Factors” and “Forward-Looking Statements” sections included in Prudential Financial, Inc.’s Annual Reports on Form 10-K and Quarterly Reports on Form 10-Q. Each of our forward-looking statements contained herein is subject to the risk that we will be unable to execute our strategy and other risks. In addition, our statements under the heading “Seasonality of Key Financial Items” are subject to the risk that different earnings and expense patterns will emerge. Prudential Financial, Inc. does not undertake to update any particular forward-looking statement included in this presentation.
____________________________________________________________________________
Prudential Financial, Inc. of the United States is not affiliated with Prudential plc which is headquartered in the United Kingdom.

### Slide 21

**Text**

NON-GAAP MEASURES / This presentation includes references to adjusted operating income. Consolidated adjusted operating income is not calculated based on accounting principles generally accepted / in the United States of America (GAAP). For additional information about adjusted operating income and adjusted book value and the comparable GAAP measures, including / reconciliations between the comparable measures, please refer to our quarterly results news releases, which are available on our website at investor.prudential.com. / Reconciliations are also included as part of this presentation. / We believe that our use of these non-GAAP measures helps investors understand and evaluate the Company’s performance and financial position. The presentation of adjusted / operating income as we measure it for management purposes enhances the understanding of the results of operations by highlighting the results from ongoing operations and the / underlying profitability of our businesses. Trends in the underlying profitability of our businesses can be more clearly identified without the fluctuating effects of the items / described below. Adjusted book value augments the understanding of our financial position by providing a measure of net worth that is primarily attributable to our business / operations separate from the portion that is affected by capital and currency market conditions, and by isolating the accounting impact associated with insurance liabilities that / are generally not marked to market and the supporting investments that are marked to market through accumulated other comprehensive income under GAAP. However, these / non-GAAP measures are not substitutes for income and equity determined in accordance with GAAP, and the adjustments made to derive these measures are important to an / understanding of our overall results of operations and financial position. The schedules accompanying this presentation provide reconciliations of non-GAAP measures with the / corresponding measures calculated using GAAP. Additional historic information relating to our financial performance is located on our website at investor.prudential.com. / Adjusted operating income is a non-GAAP measure used by the Company to evaluate segment performance and to allocate resources. Adjusted operating income excludes / “Realized investment gains (losses), net, and related charges and adjustments”. A significant element of realized investment gains and losses are impairments and credit-related / and interest rate-related gains and losses. Impairments and losses from sales of credit-impaired securities, the timing of which depends largely on market credit cycles, can vary / considerably across periods. The timing of other sales that would result in gains or losses, such as interest rate-related gains or losses, is largely subject to our discretion and / influenced by market opportunities as well as capital and other factors. / 21

Body text in medium-weight sans-serif in blue #0070C0, roughly 2.0% of slide height, with about 2.9% line pitch. Page number in light grey-blue #7E8FA1, about 1.3% of slide height.

### Slide 22

**Text**

NON-GAAP MEASURES (CONTINUED)
Realized investment gains (losses) within certain businesses for which such gains (losses) are a principal source of earnings, and those associated with terminating hedges of foreign currency earnings and current period yield adjustments, are included in adjusted operating income. Adjusted operating income generally excludes realized investment gains and losses from products that contain embedded derivatives, and from associated derivative portfolios that are part of an asset-liability management program related to the risk of those products. Adjusted operating income also excludes gains and losses from changes in value of certain assets and liabilities relating to foreign currency exchange movements that have been economically hedged or considered part of our capital funding strategies for our international subsidiaries, as well as gains and losses on certain investments that are designated as trading. Adjusted operating income also excludes investment gains and losses on assets supporting experience-rated contractholder liabilities and changes in experience-rated contractholder liabilities due to asset value changes, because these recorded changes in asset and liability values are expected to ultimately accrue to contractholders. Additionally, adjusted operating income excludes the changes in fair value of equity securities that are recorded in net income. Additionally, adjusted operating income excludes the impact of annual assumption updates and other refinements included in the above items.
Adjusted operating income excludes “Change in value of market risk benefits, net of related hedging gains (losses)”, which reflects the impact from changes in current market conditions, and market experience updates, reflecting the immediate impacts in current period results from changes in current market conditions on estimates of profitability, which we believe enhances the understanding of underlying performance trends. Adjusted operating income also excludes the results of Divested and Run-off Businesses, which are not relevant to our ongoing operations, and discontinued operations and earnings attributable to noncontrolling interests, each of which is presented as a separate component of net income under GAAP. Additionally, adjusted operating income excludes other items, such as certain components of the consideration for acquisitions, which are recognized as compensation expense over the requisite service periods, and goodwill impairments. Earnings attributable to noncontrolling interests is presented as a separate component of net income under GAAP and excluded from adjusted operating income. The tax effect associated with pre-tax adjusted operating income is based on applicable IRS and foreign tax regulations inclusive of pertinent adjustments.
Adjusted operating income does not equate to “Net income” as determined in accordance with U.S. GAAP. Adjusted operating income is not a substitute for income determined in accordance with U.S. GAAP, and our definition of adjusted operating income may differ from that used by other companies. The items above are important to an understanding of our overall results of operations. However, we believe that the presentation of adjusted operating income as we measure it for management purposes enhances the understanding of our results of operations by highlighting the results from ongoing operations and the underlying profitability of our businesses. Trends in the underlying profitability of our businesses can be more clearly identified without the fluctuating effects of the items described above.
